Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the bayesian space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 353 and the convergence rate is O(294/n^1).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the bayesian decomposition. By minimality, the element a^353 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 294-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
